- kind: review_question
  text: Verify whether any subsequent corrigenda modify the financial thresholds or work experience values specified
    in Section C EQC Clause 3.1.
  evidence_ids:
  - J704434795075ef24
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C9670a32e8b57a07f
  citations:
  - id: J704434795075ef24
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC.md
    okf_sha256: 5ae5ea7a30496ffbb3384dfb998e3fdd89ef3222744b6b639e3d564508b11172
    heading: Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 32
    - 33
    - 34
    - 35
    - 36
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: d8207622663bc2dd0cf2a7c5e3ed660f5c3881838e376d1bbb26fdae47ed8abf
    json_pointer: /clauses/2
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"3.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"\
      3.0 \\nQUALIFICATION CRITERIA \\n \\nPursuant to clause 2.0, tenderers shall meet the qualification criteria\
      \ stipulated in the succeeding paragraphs in order to qualify. \\n3.1 WORK EXPERIENCE & FINANCIAL STANDING:\
      \ \\n3.1. A. WORK EXPERIENCE \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\n3.1.A.(i)\
      \ \\nTenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears\
      \ ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/\
      \ **substantially completed One work of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of the work is equal to or exceed\
      \ ₹ 3.79 Crores.*** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (ii) \\nTenderer\
      \ as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on\
      \ last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Two works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 2.37 Crores.\
      \ *** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iii) Tenderer as a Prime\
      \ Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day\
      \ of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Three works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 1.90 Crores.***\
      \ \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iv) Tenderer as a Prime Contractor\
      \ (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical\
      \ Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"should have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed in any number of works of *Similar nature, \\nwhere the combined value of works is equal to or\
      \ exceed ₹ 4.74 Crores.*** \\nNotes: \\n1) \\n* Similar nature work for this contract shall be, ‘Demolition/Dismantling\
      \ of Buildings/Structures’. \\n2) \\n** Similar nature work(s) where Financial value of physically executed\
      \ work is 90%  or more of the original awarded contract value or \\nrevised contract value (if any), duly\
      \ certified by the client, will be treated as substantially completed Similar nature work(s) and will be \\\
      nconsidered for qualification of work experience criteria. In case original contract value has been revised,\
      \ the revised contract value shall \\nbe considered for this purpose.  \\n3) \\nThe Tenderer shall furnish\
      \ the Client’s Completion Certificate in support of the Work Experience criteria as specified under Clause\
      \ 3.1A. \\nThe certificate shall clearly indicate the Nature and Scope of Similar Nature Work, Awarded Contract\
      \ Value (excluding GST), Actual \\nCompletion Value (excluding GST), and the Actual Date of Completion. Where\
      \ applicable, the certificate shall also specify the Revised \\nContract Value (excluding GST), along with\
      \ details of any variation orders and extensions of time granted. \\n4) \\nIn cases where the Client’s Completion\
      \ Certificate indicates values inclusive of GST and/or other applicable taxes, the Tenderer shall \\nsubmit\
      \ the actual completion cost after deducting the applicable GST dully certified by Chartered Accountant. For\
      \ portions of Similar \\nNature Works executed in the pre-GST period, the Tenderer shall make a deduction\
      \ towards taxes at the rate of 12% to arrive at the \\ncompletion cost exclusive of taxes. \\n5) \\nIf a Tenderer\
      \ has completed /substantially completed the Similar Nature Work(s) as a member of a Joint Venture (JV), the\
      \ value of \\n‘Similar Nature Work(s)’ will be recognized only in proportion to the Tenderer’s stipulated\
      \ financial share in that JV. A copy of the JV \\nAgreement must be submitted with the bid. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, value of works in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per\
      \ the following procedure: \\nValue of completed/ substantially completed Similar Nature Work(s) in foreign\
      \ currency shall be converted to Equivalent INR by adopting \\nExchange rate between the foreign currency\
      \ and INR as prevailing on the date of completion / date given in the substantial completion \\ncertificate\
      \ as applicable, as per the website of Reserve Bank of India (RBI). \\n7) \\n***The value of Completed Similar\
      \ Nature Work(s) executed during the previous financial years shall be updated at a rate of 5% per \\nyear,\
      \ compounded annually, for the Indian Rupees portion (including foreign currencies, converted to Equivalent\
      \ INR), to bring them to \\nthe price level of the financial year of this Tender Invitation.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\
      \ Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit\
      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn case Audited Balance\
      \ sheet of the last financial year ending with 2025-2026 is not made available by the Tenderer, he has to\
      \ submit \\nan affidavit certifying that 'The Balance Sheet has actually not been audited so far'. In such\
      \ a case the financial data of previous '4' \\naudited financial years will be taken into consideration for\
      \ evaluation. If audited Balance sheet of any year other than the last financial \\nyear ending with 2025-2026\
      \ is not submitted even when requested for as a clarification under clause 20 of ITT, then the Tender will\
      \ be \\nconsidered as non-responsive. \\n5) \\nAll documents shall reflect the stand-alone financial status\
      \ of the Tenderer and not the Tenderer’s parent companies, subsidiaries, or \\naffiliates. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, amounts in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following\
      \ procedure: \\nIn cases where the financial data is denominated in a currency other than Indian Rupees, it\
      \ shall be converted into the equivalent value \\nin Indian Rupees by adopting the exchange rate between the\
      \ foreign currency and INR prevailing on the last day of the respective \\nfinancial year, as published on\
      \ the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) website, for the purpose of bid evaluation. \\n7) \\nTo align a January–December\
      \ financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the following conversion: \\nA calendar year\
      \ (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g., FY 2023–24, covering April\
      \ 2023 to \\nMarch 2024). Apply this rule consistently to all such conversions. \\n8) \\n****Financial turnover\
      \ in INR/converted equivalent INR of previous financial years shall be given a weightage of 5% per year \\\
      n(compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation. \\\
      n9) \\nAll the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July, 2019,\
      \ must contain UDIN thereon and \\nthe particulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification\
      \ and submitted to ICAI on its website which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\
      \ \\n3.2 BID CAPACITY: \\nRequirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nAvailable Bid capacity should\
      \ be ≥ ₹ 4.74 Crores \\nFormula: \\nAvailable Bid Capacity = (1.5 × A × N) – B  \\nAttachment-7, Attachment-\\\
      n8 to Letter of Technical Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"Requirement \\\
      nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nWhere: \\nA = Maximum value of total Civil Engineering works executed\
      \ (annual turnover from Civil Engineering works) \\nin any one year during the last five financial years.\
      \ The turnover for previous years shall be updated by 5% \\nper year (compounded annually) to bring them to\
      \ the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation.  \\nN = Number of years prescribed for\
      \ completion of this tendered work as mentioned in NIT.  \\nB = Total value (updated to the price level of\
      \ the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation) of existing \\ncommitments for ongoing Civil Engineering works\
      \ during the completion period mentioned in NIT, from the \\nlast day of the month previous to the month of\
      \ invitation of this tender. \\nNotes: \\n1) Works in Hand information provided in ‘Attachment-7 to Letter\
      \ of Technical Bid’ must be certified either by the Independent Financial \\nAuditor (Statutory Auditor) of\
      \ the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature\
      \ \\nwith Membership Number and Firm’s Registration Number. \\n2) All the documents or certifications, which\
      \ are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and the \\nparticulars\
      \ of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website,\
      \ which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\"\
      : \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Qualification Criteria\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match

    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 46,\n  \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -6 \\nSummary of Information provided in ATTACHMENT\

      \ \\ncommence\\nment \\nof  \\nwork \\nOriginal \\nDate \\nof \\ncompletion \\nof work \\nFinal date of \\\
      ncompletion \\nof work  \\nContract \\nValue \\n(at \\nthe \\ntime \\nof \\naward)  \\nComplet\\ned \\ncontract\
      \ \\nvalue  in \\nINR \\nUpdated \\nValue \\nof \\ncompleted \\ncontract in \\nEquivalent \\nINR  \\nValue\
      \ \\nof \\nSubstantially \\ncompleted \\ncontract \\nin \\nEquivalent \\nINR \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\\
      n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n\
      \ \\n \\n \\n \\nNotes: \\n \\n1. Value of completed Similar Nature Work(s) executed shall be updated to the\
      \ price level of the financial year in which the tenders are invited as per table \\ngiven below: \\nFinancial\
      \ year \\n2019-20 \\n2020-21 \\n2021-2022 \\n2022-2023 \\n2023-2024 \\n2024-2025 \\n2025-2026 \\n2026-2027\
      \ \\nIndian Currency \\n1.41 \\n1.34 \\n1.28 \\n1.22 \\n1.16 \\n1.10 \\n1.05 \\n1.00 \\n \\n2. Value of completed/\
      \ substantially completed Similar Nature Work(s) in foreign currency shall be converted to Equivalent INR\
      \ by adopting Exchange rate \\nbetween the foreign currency and INR as prevailing on the date of completion\
      \ / date given in the substantial completion certificate as applicable, as per the \\nwebsite of Reserve Bank\
      \ of India (RBI). \\n \\n3. In case the work of Similar Nature is executed by the Joint venture, the value\
      \ of Similar Nature of work executed shall be considered as per percentage \\nparticipation by the member(s)\
      \ in that Joint venture/consortium. \\n \\n(Signature) …………………… \\nName of signatory: ……………………………… \\nCapacity\
      \ of signatory: …………………………… \\nName and address of Tenderer: ……………….\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 47,\n  \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -7 \\n             Works in Hand \\n            (To\

      \ \\nCompletion Date \\n \\n *The updated value (**in INR) \\nof \\nthe \\nbalance \\nCivil \\nEngineering\
      \  works remaining \\nto be executed during the \\ncompletion period specified in \\nthe NIT of this tender,\
      \ as on the \\nlast day of the month previous \\nto the month of invitation of \\nthis tender  \\n \\n \\\
      n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\nTotal \\n \\n \\nNote:  \\n1. * For calculation of Updated Balance work to\
      \ the price level of the financial year in which this tender is invited, assume inflation as per multiplying\
      \ Factors given in \\nthe table under note 1 of Attachment-6. \\n2. Tenderer should provide information on\
      \ their current commitments or all contracts that have been awarded or for which a letter of intent or acceptance\
      \ has been \\nreceived or for contracts approaching completion but for which a completion certificate is yet\
      \ to be issued. \\n3. **Total Equivalent INR for balance work in foreign currency should be calculated using\
      \ the selling exchange rate of Reserve Bank of India as on the last day of the \\nmonth previous to the month\
      \ of invitation of this tender. In case publication is not available on the above day, previous working day\
      \ published exchange rate shall \\nbe considered for evaluation. \\n4. Works in Hand information must be certified\
      \ either by the Independent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed under the Companies’\
      \ Act or \\nby a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature in original with membership number and\
      \ firm registration number.\"\n}"

    - 1

    json_path: parsed_json/addendum_corrigendum/corrigenda_amendments.json
    json_sha256: 41af12b27292ebbdcf0de3bbd3c72bf1a52ac20ad7977041d1ed344c3240e712
    json_pointer: /amendments/5
    json_record_id: corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"affected_reference\": \"12.3.1, (iii) c\",\n  \"affected_section\": \"GCC\",\n  \"booklet_page_reference\"\
      : \"121 & 122\",\n  \"corrigendum_number\": 2,\n  \"document_date\": \"2026-10-08\",\n  \"existing_selected_text\"\
      : [\n    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and in excess of 100% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor\
      \ value item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities\
      \ operated in excess of 100% but upto 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall\
      \ be paid at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities\
      \ of individual minor value item beyond 200% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per\
      \ accepted rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"field_provenance\": \"Prepared from visual inspection of the source scan; not\
      \ automatically inferred from OCR\",\n  \"ocr_evidence_relative_to_project\": \"output/corrigenda/ocr/run_20261030T051229372457Z/corrigendum_2/ocr.json\"\
      ,\n  \"operation\": \"replace_specified_minor_value_item_passage\",\n  \"pdf_pages\": [\n    1\n  ],\n  \"\
      printed_amendment_serial\": \"1\",\n  \"record_id\": \"corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation\",\n \
      \ \"replacement_rate_bands\": [\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 100,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\"\
      : 200\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 98,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\"\
      : 200,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\": 300\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\"\
      : 96,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\": 300\n    }\n  ],\n  \"replacement_selected_text\": [\n\
      \    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and including 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value\
      \ item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities operated\
      \ in excess of 200% but upto 300% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall be paid\
      \ at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities of\
      \ individual minor value item beyond 300% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted\
      \ rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_notes\": [\n    \"Preserve the unusual existing wording 'upto and in excess of\
      \ 100%'; do not silently repair it.\",\n    \"The replacement rate bands are derived structured fields.\"\
      ,\n    \"The surrounding unchanged passage remains in the OCR.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      ,\n  \"source_document\": \"AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF\",\n  \"source_sha256\": \"db6b30a513be4294cda22c931729ee418297a59dce4459fee508c8061a10d77a\"\
      ,\n  \"transcription_scope\": \"Selected amendment wording and fields, not a complete verbatim table transcription\"\
      ,\n  \"unchanged_selected_text\": \"A minor value item for this purpose is defined as an item whose original\
      \ agreement value is less than 1 % of the total original contract value.\"\n}"

      — Contract Price and Payment]]

      - **Deletion scope:** The entirety of the listed GCC subclauses

      - **Evidence:** Corrigendum PDF page 2, amendment serial 3

    quote: '- **Amends:** [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE#Contract Data|Contract Data Item 14]]

      - **Existing label:** NOT APPLICABLE

      - **Replacement label:** NOT APPLICABLE

      - **Existing GCC reference:** 11.1.2

      - **Replacement GCC references:** 11.1.2, 11.1.2.1, 11.1.2.2, 11.1.2.3 and 11.1.2.4

      clause-reference list.'

    json_pointer: /amendments/5
    json_record_id: corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"affected_reference\": \"12.3.1, (iii) c\",\n  \"affected_section\": \"GCC\",\n  \"booklet_page_reference\"\
      : \"121 & 122\",\n  \"corrigendum_number\": 2,\n  \"document_date\": \"2026-10-08\",\n  \"existing_selected_text\"\
      : [\n    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and in excess of 100% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor\
      \ value item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities\
      \ operated in excess of 100% but upto 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall\
      \ be paid at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities\
      \ of individual minor value item beyond 200% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per\
      \ accepted rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"field_provenance\": \"Prepared from visual inspection of the source scan; not\
      \ automatically inferred from OCR\",\n  \"ocr_evidence_relative_to_project\": \"output/corrigenda/ocr/run_20261030T051229372457Z/corrigendum_2/ocr.json\"\
      ,\n  \"operation\": \"replace_specified_minor_value_item_passage\",\n  \"pdf_pages\": [\n    1\n  ],\n  \"\
      printed_amendment_serial\": \"1\",\n  \"record_id\": \"corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation\",\n \
      \ \"replacement_rate_bands\": [\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 100,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\"\
      : 200\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 98,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\"\
      : 200,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\": 300\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\"\
      : 96,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\": 300\n    }\n  ],\n  \"replacement_selected_text\": [\n\
      \    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and including 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value\
      \ item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities operated\
      \ in excess of 200% but upto 300% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall be paid\
      \ at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities of\
      \ individual minor value item beyond 300% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted\
      \ rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_notes\": [\n    \"Preserve the unusual existing wording 'upto and in excess of\
      \ 100%'; do not silently repair it.\",\n    \"The replacement rate bands are derived structured fields.\"\
      ,\n    \"The surrounding unchanged passage remains in the OCR.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      ,\n  \"source_document\": \"AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF\",\n  \"source_sha256\": \"db6b30a513be4294cda22c931729ee418297a59dce4459fee508c8061a10d77a\"\
      ,\n  \"transcription_scope\": \"Selected amendment wording and fields, not a complete verbatim table transcription\"\
      ,\n  \"unchanged_selected_text\": \"A minor value item for this purpose is defined as an item whose original\
      \ agreement value is less than 1 % of the total original contract value.\"\n}"

    quote: '- Corrigendum PDF page numbers and affected original booklet-page numbers are different references.

      - The SCC additions specify deletion of the listed subclauses, not deletion of the entire parent GCC clauses.

      - The added SCC numbering must not be confused with GCC numbering.

      - Original GCC wording should remain available as historical evidence.

      - The original text of each deleted GCC provision is not reproduced in the amendment row.

      - Final transcription and applicability review remains pending.

      >>>>>>> b1f5c9eb1f3986f540500a5c5ee639e92e9edc53'

- kind: review_question
  text: Verify the operational and legal impact of Corrigendum No. 2 deleting multiple major GCC sub-clauses (sub-contracting
    under 4.5, early completion incentives under 8.10, price variation under 11.1.2 series, mobilization and equipment
    advances under 11.2 series, special/acceleration advance under 11.2.3, and professional indemnity insurance
    under 15.1) while keeping the Contract Data label for Price Variation as NOT APPLICABLE with an expanded clause-reference
    list.

    json_pointer: /amendments/5
    json_record_id: corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"affected_reference\": \"12.3.1, (iii) c\",\n  \"affected_section\": \"GCC\",\n  \"booklet_page_reference\"\
      : \"121 & 122\",\n  \"corrigendum_number\": 2,\n  \"document_date\": \"2026-10-08\",\n  \"existing_selected_text\"\
      : [\n    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and in excess of 100% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor\
      \ value item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities\
      \ operated in excess of 100% but upto 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall\
      \ be paid at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities\
      \ of individual minor value item beyond 200% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per\
      \ accepted rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"field_provenance\": \"Prepared from visual inspection of the source scan; not\
      \ automatically inferred from OCR\",\n  \"ocr_evidence_relative_to_project\": \"output/corrigenda/ocr/run_20261030T051229372457Z/corrigendum_2/ocr.json\"\
      ,\n  \"operation\": \"replace_specified_minor_value_item_passage\",\n  \"pdf_pages\": [\n    1\n  ],\n  \"\
      printed_amendment_serial\": \"1\",\n  \"record_id\": \"corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation\",\n \
      \ \"replacement_rate_bands\": [\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 100,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\"\
      : 200\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 98,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\"\
      : 200,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\": 300\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\"\
      : 96,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\": 300\n    }\n  ],\n  \"replacement_selected_text\": [\n\
      \    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and including 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value\
      \ item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities operated\
      \ in excess of 200% but upto 300% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall be paid\
      \ at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities of\
      \ individual minor value item beyond 300% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted\
      \ rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_notes\": [\n    \"Preserve the unusual existing wording 'upto and in excess of\
      \ 100%'; do not silently repair it.\",\n    \"The replacement rate bands are derived structured fields.\"\
      ,\n    \"The surrounding unchanged passage remains in the OCR.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      ,\n  \"source_document\": \"AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF\",\n  \"source_sha256\": \"db6b30a513be4294cda22c931729ee418297a59dce4459fee508c8061a10d77a\"\
      ,\n  \"transcription_scope\": \"Selected amendment wording and fields, not a complete verbatim table transcription\"\
      ,\n  \"unchanged_selected_text\": \"A minor value item for this purpose is defined as an item whose original\
      \ agreement value is less than 1 % of the total original contract value.\"\n}"

  - id: S7b99d9f12d93c1c0
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C2.md
    okf_sha256: cdd5f8641206e173e0b62d494966695a4d54c893ce8b0cd781d5a9c3837ca161
    heading: 'Change 5 — Add SCC Clause 6: Price Variation/Adjustment'
    source_document: AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF
    pdf_pages:
    - 2
    quote: '- **Change type:** Add an SCC clause containing GCC deletion instructions

      - **New SCC clause:** 6

      - **Target GCC subclauses:** 11.1.2, 11.1.2.1, 11.1.2.2, 11.1.2.3 and 11.1.2.4

      - **GCC navigation:** [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE#Clause 11 — Contract Price and Payment|GCC Clause 11
      — Contract Price and Payment]]

      - **Instruction:** Entire Sub-clauses stands DELETED

      - **Deletion scope:** The entirety of the listed GCC subclauses

      - **Evidence:** Corrigendum PDF page 2, amendment serial 3


      This addition should be read alongside Change 2, which retains NOT APPLICABLE in Contract Data Item 14.'
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference
  - id: Sfd23c1747d11bf6b
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C2.md
    okf_sha256: cdd5f8641206e173e0b62d494966695a4d54c893ce8b0cd781d5a9c3837ca161
    heading: 'Change 2 — Contract Data Item 14: Price Variation/Adjustment'
    source_document: AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF
    pdf_pages:
    - 1
    quote: '- **Amends:** [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE#Contract Data|Contract Data Item 14]]

      - **Change type:** Replace the referenced GCC clause list

      - **Existing label:** NOT APPLICABLE

      - **Replacement label:** NOT APPLICABLE

      - **Existing GCC reference:** 11.1.2

      - **Replacement GCC references:** 11.1.2, 11.1.2.1, 11.1.2.2, 11.1.2.3 and 11.1.2.4

      - **Evidence:** `AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF`, PDF page 1, amendment serial 2

      - **Affected booklet pages:** 136 and 137


      The replacement does not make price variation applicable. The NOT APPLICABLE label remains, with an expanded
      clause-reference list.'
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference

    pdf_pages:
    - 1
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/addendum_corrigendum/corrigenda_amendments.json
    json_sha256: 41af12b27292ebbdcf0de3bbd3c72bf1a52ac20ad7977041d1ed344c3240e712
    json_pointer: /amendments/5
    json_record_id: corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"affected_reference\": \"12.3.1, (iii) c\",\n  \"affected_section\": \"GCC\",\n  \"booklet_page_reference\"\
      : \"121 & 122\",\n  \"corrigendum_number\": 2,\n  \"document_date\": \"2026-10-08\",\n  \"existing_selected_text\"\
      : [\n    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and in excess of 100% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor\
      \ value item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities\
      \ operated in excess of 100% but upto 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall\
      \ be paid at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities\
      \ of individual minor value item beyond 200% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per\
      \ accepted rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"field_provenance\": \"Prepared from visual inspection of the source scan; not\
      \ automatically inferred from OCR\",\n  \"ocr_evidence_relative_to_project\": \"output/corrigenda/ocr/run_20261030T051229372457Z/corrigendum_2/ocr.json\"\
      ,\n  \"operation\": \"replace_specified_minor_value_item_passage\",\n  \"pdf_pages\": [\n    1\n  ],\n  \"\
      printed_amendment_serial\": \"1\",\n  \"record_id\": \"corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation\",\n \
      \ \"replacement_rate_bands\": [\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 100,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\"\
      : 200\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 98,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\"\
      : 200,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\": 300\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\"\
      : 96,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\": 300\n    }\n  ],\n  \"replacement_selected_text\": [\n\
      \    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and including 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value\
      \ item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities operated\
      \ in excess of 200% but upto 300% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall be paid\
      \ at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities of\
      \ individual minor value item beyond 300% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted\
      \ rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_notes\": [\n    \"Preserve the unusual existing wording 'upto and in excess of\
      \ 100%'; do not silently repair it.\",\n    \"The replacement rate bands are derived structured fields.\"\
      ,\n    \"The surrounding unchanged passage remains in the OCR.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      ,\n  \"source_document\": \"AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF\",\n  \"source_sha256\": \"db6b30a513be4294cda22c931729ee418297a59dce4459fee508c8061a10d77a\"\
      ,\n  \"transcription_scope\": \"Selected amendment wording and fields, not a complete verbatim table transcription\"\
      ,\n  \"unchanged_selected_text\": \"A minor value item for this purpose is defined as an item whose original\
      \ agreement value is less than 1 % of the total original contract value.\"\n}"

    okf_path: OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C2.md

    heading: 'Change 5 — Add SCC Clause 6: Price Variation/Adjustment'
    source_document: AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF
    pdf_pages:
    - 2
    quote: '- **Change type:** Add an SCC clause containing GCC deletion instructions

      - **New SCC clause:** 6

      - **Target GCC subclauses:** 11.1.2, 11.1.2.1, 11.1.2.2, 11.1.2.3 and 11.1.2.4

      - **GCC navigation:** [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE#Clause 11 — Contract Price and Payment|GCC Clause 11
      — Contract Price and Payment]]

      - **Instruction:** Entire Sub-clauses stands DELETED

      - **Deletion scope:** The entirety of the listed GCC subclauses

      - **Evidence:** Corrigendum PDF page 2, amendment serial 3


      This addition should be read alongside Change 2, which retains NOT APPLICABLE in Contract Data Item 14.'
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference
  - id: Sfd23c1747d11bf6b

    quote: '- **Amends:** [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE#Contract Data|Contract Data Item 14]]

      - **Change type:** Replace the referenced GCC clause list

      - **Existing label:** NOT APPLICABLE

      - **Replacement label:** NOT APPLICABLE

      - **Existing GCC reference:** 11.1.2

      - **Replacement GCC references:** 11.1.2, 11.1.2.1, 11.1.2.2, 11.1.2.3 and 11.1.2.4

      - **Evidence:** `AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF`, PDF page 1, amendment serial 2

      - **Affected booklet pages:** 136 and 137


      The replacement does not make price variation applicable. The NOT APPLICABLE label remains, with an expanded
      clause-reference list.'

      27.0     Lighting:\\n\\n\\n27.1    The contractor shall provide sufficient site lighting, of the right type\
      \ and at the right\\n         place for it to be properly effective. Lighting ought not to introduce the risk\
      \ of electric\\n         shock. Therefore, 230V supplies should be used for those fittings, which are robustly\\\
      n           installed, and well out of reach e.g. flood lighting or high-pressure discharge lamps.\\n\\n27.2\
      \      Selection of Luminaries:\\n\\n        The contractor shall select the luminaries as per the area requirement\
      \ indicated below:\\n\\n            Sl.   Type of         Area of Requirement             Luminaries\\n  \
      \       No.  Lighting\\n\\n             1.   Area Lighting    Workmen and vehicles       i)  Shovel type:\
      \ non-symmetrical\\n                                      to move about in safely.     ii)  Symmetrical or\
      \ non-\\n                                                             symmetrical tungsten halogen\\n\\n \
      \            2.  Beam flood      Concentrated light over      i)  Portable flood light (Conical\\n       \
      \             lighting         an area from a            beam)\\n                                        relatively\
      \ great                     ii)  Wide angle flood (fan shaped\\n                                    distance.\
      \                 beam)\\n\\n                                                                            \
      \                                   iii)  Medium or narrow angle flood\\n                                \
      \                                (Conical beam)\\n\\n             3.   Dispersive        Lighting for indoor\
      \               i)  Dispersive (Mercury florescent)\\n                    lighting                       \
      \                                           ii)  Cargo cluster\\n\\n                                     \
      \                                                                          iii)  Florescent trough\\n\\n \
      \            4.   Walkway lighting  Lighting for stairways,         i)  Well glass unit\\n               \
      \                   ladder ways, corridors,       ii)  Bulkhead unit (tungsten filament)\\n              \
      \                       scaffold access routs, etc\\n                                                    \
      \                                                           iii)  Bulk head unit (Florescent)\\n\\n      \
      \       5.   Local lighting     Lighting on sites and fittin   i) PAR (Parabolic Aluminised\\n           \
      \                      are generally accessible t     Reflector) lamp cluster\\n                         \
      \         operatives                            ii)  Festoons (with or without shades)\\n\\n             \
      \                                                                                                  iii)  Adjustable\
      \ florescent work lamp\\n\\n                                                                             \
      \  iv)  Portable flood lamp (mounted on\\n                                                own cable drum)\\\
      n\\n\\n27.3    The contractor shall ensure that luminaries should always be placed so that no person\\n  \

      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        224\\n18.20    Requirements for Ladders\\n\\n\\n\
      \          1)    Every contractor shall ensure that a ladder is used for work at height only if\\n       \
      \      a risk assessment has demonstrated that the use of more suitable work\\n              equipment is\
      \ not justified because of the low risk and\\n\\n                          i)    The short duration of use;\
      \ or\\n\\n                            ii)    Existing features on site, which he cannot alter.\\n\\n     \
      \     2)    Only metal ladders shall be allowed. Bamboo ladders are prohibited.\\n\\n          3)   Any surface\
      \ upon which a ladder rests shall be stable, firm, of sufficient\\n                strength and of suitable\
      \ composition safely to support the ladder so that its\\n              rungs or steps remain horizontal, and\
      \ any loading intended to be placed on\\n                            it.\\n\\n          4)   A ladder shall\
      \ be so positioned as to ensure its stability during use\\n\\n          5)   A suspended ladder shall be attached\
      \ in a secure manner and so that, with\\n                the exception of a flexible ladder,  it cannot be\
      \ displaced and swinging is\\n               prevented.\\n\\n          6)   A portable ladder shall be prevented\
      \ from slipping during use by -\\n\\n                          i)     securing the stiles at or near their\
      \ upper or lower ends;\\n\\n                            ii)    an effective anti-slip or other effective stability\
      \ device; or\\n\\n                             iii)   any other arrangement of equivalent effectiveness.\\\
      n\\n          7)   A ladder used for access shall be long enough to protrude sufficiently above\\n       \
      \         the place of landing to which  it provides access, unless other measures\\n             have been\
      \ taken to ensure a firm handhold.\\n\\n          8)   No interlocking or extension ladder shall be used unless\
      \ its sections are\\n               prevented from moving relative to each other while in use.\\n\\n     \
      \     9)   A mobile ladder shall be prevented from moving before it is stepped on.\\n\\n         10)  Where\
      \ a ladder or run of ladders raises a vertical distance of 9 metres or\\n             more above its base,\
      \ there shall, where reasonably practicable, be provided\\n                 at suitable intervals sufficient\
      \ safe landing areas or rest platforms.\\n\\n         11)  Every ladder shall be used in such a way that\\\
      n\\n                a)   a secure handhold and secure support are always available to the\\n             \
      \         user; and\\n\\n                b)    the user can maintain a safe handhold when carrying a load\
      \ unless, in\\n                     the case of a step ladder, the maintenance of a handhold  is not\\n  \
      \                     practicable when a load  is  carried, and a  risk assessment has\\n                \
      \   demonstrated that the use of a stepladder is justified because of\\n\\n                              \
      \      i)     the low risk; and\\n\\n                                       ii)    the short duration of use.\\\
      n\\n\\n19.0    Overhead protection\\n\\n\\n            All contractors shall provide overhead protections\
      \ as per Rule 41 of BOCWR\\n\\n                i)    Overhead protection should be erected along the periphery\
      \ of every building\\n              which is under construction and the building height shall be 15m or above\\\
      n                  after construction.\\n\\n                  ii)    Overhead protection shall be minimum\
      \ 2m wide and the outer edge shall be\\n           150mm higher than the inner edge and an angle not more\
      \ than 200 to its\\n                 horizontal sloping into the building.\\n\\n                  iii)   Overhead\
      \ protection shall not be erected more than a height of 5m from the\\n             base of the building.\\\
      n\\n             iv)   Areas of inadvertent hazard of  falling of material  shall be guarded or\\n       \
      \        barricaded or roped-off thereby by the contractor.\\n\\n\\n20.0     Slipping, Tripping, Cutting,\
      \ Drowning and Falling Hazards\\n\\n        As per Rule 42 of BOCWR,\\n\\n                i)      All places\
      \ should be free from dust, debris or similar materials.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.\

      \   Time office, canteen, drinking water,  toilet and rest place shall be suitably\\n                located\
      \  for the easy access to workers.  All the  facilities shall be properly\\n              cleaned and maintained\
      \ during the entire period of operation.\\n\\n              vii)   Manual handling of cement shall be avoided\
      \ to a larger extent. Whenever  it is\\n                absolutely necessary the workmen shall be given  full\
      \ body protection, hand\\n                protection and respiratory protection as a basic measure of ensuring\
      \ better\\n                 health.\\n\\n               viii)  The PPEs provided to cement handling workmen\
      \ shall conform to international\\n               standards.\\n\\n             ix)   Access roads and internal\
      \ circulation roads shall be well laid and maintained\\n                properly at all time.\\n\\n      \
      \     x)    Non-adherence to any of the above provision shall be penalised as per relevant\\n            \
      \    penalty clause.\\n\\n39.0     Personal Protective Equipments (PPEs)\\n\\n\\n39.1    The contractor shall\
      \ provide required PPEs to workmen to protect against safety and /\\n          or health hazards. Primarily\
      \ PPEs are required for the following protection\\n\\n\\n                i)    Head Protection (Safety helmets)\\\
      n                  ii)    Foot Protection (Safety footwear, Gumboot, etc)\\n                  iii)   Body\
      \ Protection (High visibility clothing (waistcoat/jacket), Apron, etc)\\n             iv)   Personal fall\
      \ protection (Full body harness, Rope-grap fall arrester, etc)\\n           v)   Eye Protection (Goggles,\
      \ Welders glasses, etc)\\n             vi)   Hand Protection (Gloves, Finger coats, etc)\\n              vii)\
      \   Respiratory Protection. (Nose mask, SCBAs, etc)\\n               viii)  Hearing Protection (Ear plugs,\
      \ Ear muffs, etc)\\n\\n\\n39.2    The PPEs and safety appliances provided by the contractor shall be of the\\\
      n         standard as prescribed by Bureau of Indian Standards  (BIS).  If materials\\n        conforming\
      \ to BIS standards are not available, the contractor as approved by\\n         the Employer shall procure\
      \ PPE and safety appliances.\\n\\n\\n39.3      All construction workers should be provided with high visibility\
      \ jackets with\\n          reflective tapes confirming to the requirement specified under BS EN 471: 1994\\\
      n        as most of viaduct /tunnelling/station and depot works are executed either\\n        above or under\
      \ right-of-way. The conspicuity of workmen at all times shall be\\n         increased so as to protect them\
      \ from speeding vehicular traffic.\\n\\n39.4    The contractor shall provide safety helmet, safety shoe and\
      \ high visibility clothing\\n            for all employees including workmen, traffic marshal and other employees\
      \ who are\\n        engaged for any work under this contract as per the following requirement.\\n\\n\\n\\\
      n\\n\\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 56 of 114\",\n \

      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"v) \\nDrainage shall be effectively provided and\
      \ waste water shall be disposed after \\nproper treatment \\nvi) \\nTime office, canteen, drinking water,\
      \ toilet and rest place shall be suitably \\nlocated for the easy access to workers. All the facilities shall\
      \ be properly \\ncleaned and maintained during the entire period of operation. \\nvii) \\nManual handling\
      \ of cement shall be avoided to a larger extent. Whenever it is \\nabsolutely necessary the workmen shall\
      \ be given full body protection, hand \\nprotection and respiratory protection as a basic measure of ensuring\
      \ better \\nhealth. \\nviii) \\nThe PPEs provided to cement handling workmen shall conform to international\
      \ \\nstandards. \\nix) \\nAccess roads and internal circulation roads shall be well laid and maintained \\\
      nproperly at all time. \\nx) \\nNon-adherence to any of the above provision shall be penalised as per relevant\
      \  \\npenalty clause. \\n39.0 \\nPersonal Protective Equipments (PPEs) \\n \\n39.1 \\nThe contractor shall\
      \ provide required PPEs to workmen to protect against safety and / \\nor health hazards. Primarily PPEs are\
      \ required for the following protection \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        82.43856048583984,\n\
      \        283.4997863769531,\n        512.7667846679688,\n        765.4119873046875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \" \\ni) \\nHead Protection (Safety helmets) \\nii) \\nFoot Protection (Safety footwear,\
      \ Gumboot, etc) \\niii) \\nBody Protection (High visibility clothing (waistcoat/jacket), Apron, etc) \\niv)\
      \ \\nPersonal fall protection (Full body harness, Rope-grap fall arrester, etc) \\nv) \\nEye Protection (Goggles,\
      \ Welders glasses, etc) \\nvi) \\nHand Protection (Gloves, Finger coats, etc) \\nvii) \\nRespiratory Protection.\
      \ (Nose mask, SCBAs, etc)  \\nviii) \\nHearing Protection (Ear plugs, Ear muffs, etc) \\n \\n39.2 \\nThe PPEs\
      \ and safety appliances provided by the contractor shall be of the \\nstandard as prescribed by Bureau of\
      \ Indian Standards (BIS). If materials \\nconforming to BIS standards are not available, the contractor as\
      \ approved by \\nthe Employer shall procure PPE and safety appliances. \\n \\n39.3 \\nAll construction workers\
      \ should be provided with high visibility jackets with \\nreflective tapes confirming to the requirement specified\
      \ under BS EN 471: 1994 \\nas most of viaduct /tunnelling/station and depot works are executed either \\nabove\
      \ or under right-of-way. The conspicuity of workmen at all times shall be \\nincreased so as to protect them\
      \ from speeding vehicular traffic. \\n39.4 \\nThe contractor shall provide safety helmet, safety shoe and\
      \ high visibility clothing \\nfor all employees including workmen, traffic marshal and other employees who\
      \ are \\nengaged for any work under this contract as per the following requirement. \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\\

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"42.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"text\": \"\
      42.0     Medical Facilities\\n\\n\\n42.1     Medical Examination\\n\\n\\n42.1.1   The contractor shall arrange\
      \ a medical examination of all his employees including his\\n          sub-contractor employees employed as\
      \ drivers, operators of  lifting appliances and\\n          transport equipment before employing, after illness\
      \ or injury,  if  it appears that the\\n           illness or injury might have affected his fitness and,\
      \ thereafter, once in every two years\\n        up to the age of 40 and once in a year, thereafter.\\n\\n\\\
      n                i)    The Contractor shall maintain the confidential records of medical examination or\\\
      n                the physician authorized by the Employer.\\n\\n                  ii)   No building or other\
      \ construction worker is charged for the medical examination\\n             and the cost of such examination\
      \ is borne by contractor employing such building\\n               worker.\\n\\n                  iii)   The\
      \ medical examination shall include: -\\n\\n\\n                a)     Full medical and occupational history.\\\
      n\\n                b)     Clinical examination with particular reference to\\n\\n                       \
      \             i)    General Physique;\\n\\n                                       ii)     Vision:- Total visual\
      \ performance using standard orthorator  like\\n                         Titmus  Vision  Tester  should be\
      \  estimated and  suitability  for\\n                         placement  ascertained  in accordance  with\
      \  the  prescribed  job\\n                           standards.\\n\\n                                    \
      \    iii)    Hearing:  - Persons with normal must be able to hear a forced\\n                          whisper\
      \ at twenty-four feet. Persons using hearing aids must be\\n                           able to hear a warning\
      \ shout under noisy working conditions.\\n\\n                             iv)    Breathing:- Peak flow rate\
      \ using standard peak flow meter and the\\n                        average peak flow rate determined out of\
      \ these readings of the test\\n                          performed.  The  results  recorded  at  pre-placement\
      \  medical\\n                          examination could be used as a standard for the same individual at\\\
      n                           the same altitude for reference during subsequent examination.\\n\\n         \
      \               v)   Upper Limbs: - Adequate arm function and grip\\n\\n                             vi) \
      \   Spine: - Adequately flexible for the job concerned.\\n\\n                               vii)   Lower Limbs:\
      \ - Adequate leg and foot concerned.\\n\\n                                 viii)   General: - Mental alertness\
      \ and stability with good eye, hand and\\n                              foot coordination.\\n\\n         \
      \        c)   Any other tests which the examining doctor considers necessary\\n\\n\\n42.1.2.     If the contractor\
      \ fails to get the medical examination conducted as mentioned above,\\n          the employer will have the\
      \ right to get the same conducted by through an agency with\\n           intimation to the contractor and\
      \ deduct the cost and overhead charges.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"text\": \"42.2\
      \     Occupational Health Centre\\n\\n          42.2.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site\
      \ an occupational health centre,\\n                 mobile or static is provided and maintained in good order.\
      \ Services and facilities\\n                as per the scale lay down in Schedule X of BOCWR. A construction\
      \ medical\\n                     officer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification\
      \ as\\n                     laid down in Schedule XI of BOCWR.\\n\\n\\n          42.3    Ambulance van and\
      \ room\\n\\n\\n          42.3.1   The contractor  shall ensure  at a construction  site of a building or other\\\
      n                  construction work that an ambulance van and room are provided at such\\n              \
      \    construction site or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing\\n                such\
      \ ambulance van for transportation of serious cases of accident or sickness\\n                   of workers\
      \  to  hospital promptly and such ambulance van and room are\\n                 maintained in good repair\
      \ and is equipped with standard facilities specified in\\n                Schedule IV and Schedule V of BOCWR.\\\
      n\\n\\n          42.4      First-aid boxes\\n\\n\\n          42.4.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction\
      \ site one First-aid box for 100 workers\\n                   provided and maintained for providing First-aid\
      \ to the building workers. Every First-aid\\n                 box  is  distinctly marked  “First-aid” and\
      \  is equipped with the  articles specified  in\\n                 Schedule III of BOCWR.\\n\\n\\n       \
      \   42.5     HIV/ AIDS prevention and control\\n\\n\\n          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s\
      \ Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and\\n                    Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors”\
      \ and the copy of the policy is given in\\n                Appendix No.: 4.\\n\\n          42.5.2   The Employer\
      \ will engage a professional agency for implementing the guidelines laid\\n               down in the policy\
      \ and communicate to the contractor.\\n\\n          42.5.3   The Contractor shall extend necessary support\
      \ to the appointed agency by deputing\\n                    the workmen to attend the awareness creation programmes.\\\
      n\\n          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend necessary organizational support to the appointed\\\
      n                 agency  for the  effective implementation  of the Employers’ workplace  policy on\\n   \
      \              HIV/AIDS for workmen of the Contractors.\\n\\n          42.5.5   As laid down in the policy\
      \ the contractor shall identify peer educators (1 for every 100\\n                   workers) and refer them\
      \ for professional training to the Employers’ appointed agency\\n                       for the purpose.\\\
      n\\n          42.5.6   The peer educators on completion of the training shall serve as the focal point\\n\
      \                    for any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen\\n          \
      \       throughout the contract period.\\n\\n          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly\
      \ honorarium as fixed by the Employer for\\n                   rendering his services in addition to his regular\
      \ duty.\\n\\n          42.5.8   The total number of peer educators (1 for 100 workers) shall always be maintained\
      \ by\\n                    the contractor.\\n\\n          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave\
      \ the contractor by creating vacancy, then\\n                  the contractor at his own expense train the\
      \ new replacement peer educator from\\n                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose.\\\
      n          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor that due care should be taken to select the peer\\\
      n                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain with the contractor\\n \
      \                  throughout the contract period.\\n\\n          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"text\": \"42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito\
      \ breeding at site. The measures to be\\n         taken shall include:\\n\\n\\n                i)    Empty\
      \ cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water\\n                  shall be deposited\
      \ at a central collection point and shall be removed from the\\n                   site regularly.\\n\\n \
      \                 ii)      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent\\\
      n              mosquito breeding.\\n\\n                  iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on\
      \ the site, which may retain water,\\n                  shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner\
      \ that water could not be\\n                 retained.\\n\\n             iv)   Water storage tanks shall be\
      \ provided.\\n\\n\\n42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the\
      \ dangers\\n           of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site.\\n\\n\\\
      n42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange to prevent mosquito breeding\\n        by fumigation\
      \  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall\\n         include SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex,\
      \ The Ideal Larvicide etc.\\n\\n\\n42.7     Alcohol and drugs\\n\\n\\n42.7.1   The contractor shall ensure\
      \ at  all times that no employee  is working under the\\n          influence of alcohol / drugs which are\
      \ punishable under Govt. regulations.\\n\\n\\n42.7.2   Smoking  at  public worksites by any employee  is also\
      \ prohibited as per Govt.\\n          regulations.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"42.0     Medical\
      \ Facilities\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\": 19,\n\

      \     \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"          42.2     Occupational Health Centre\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n \
      \     \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"          42.2.1   The contractor\
      \ shall ensure at a construction site an occupational health centre,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"                 mobile or static is provided and\
      \ maintained in good order. Services and facilities\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 7,\n      \"text\": \"                as per the scale lay down in Schedule X of BOCWR. A construction medical\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"           \
      \          officer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification as\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"                     laid\
      \ down in Schedule XI of BOCWR.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"\
      text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \          42.3    Ambulance van and room\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"\
      text\": \"          42.3.1   The contractor  shall ensure  at a construction  site of a building or other\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \        construction work that an ambulance van and room are provided at such\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"                  construction site\
      \ or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"                such ambulance van for transportation\
      \ of serious cases of accident or sickness\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 19,\n      \"text\": \"                   of workers  to  hospital promptly and such ambulance van and room\
      \ are\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \              maintained in good repair and is equipped with standard facilities specified in\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"text\": \"                Schedule\
      \ IV and Schedule V of BOCWR.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"\
      text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \          42.4      First-aid boxes\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 25,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"\
      text\": \"          42.4.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site one First-aid box for 100\
      \ workers\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \                   provided and maintained for providing First-aid to the building workers. Every First-aid\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 29,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \       box  is  distinctly marked  “First-aid” and  is equipped with the  articles specified  in\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"text\": \"                 Schedule\
      \ III of BOCWR.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 31,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5\
      \     HIV/ AIDS prevention and control\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 34,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 35,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 36,\n      \"\
      text\": \"          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \          Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors” and the copy of the policy is given in\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 38,\n      \"text\": \"                Appendix\
      \ No.: 4.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 39,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"        \
      \  42.5.2   The Employer will engage a professional agency for implementing the guidelines laid\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 41,\n      \"text\": \"               down\
      \ in the policy and communicate to the contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 42,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 43,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          42.5.3   The Contractor shall extend necessary support to the appointed agency\
      \ by deputing\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 44,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                    the workmen to attend the awareness creation programmes.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend\
      \ necessary organizational support to the appointed\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 47,\n      \"text\": \"                 agency  for the  effective implementation  of the Employers’ workplace\
      \  policy on\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 48,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                 HIV/AIDS for workmen of the Contractors.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 50,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.5   As laid down in the policy the contractor\
      \ shall identify peer educators (1 for every 100\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 51,\n      \"text\": \"                   workers) and refer them for professional training to the Employers’\
      \ appointed agency\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 52,\n      \"\
      text\": \"                       for the purpose.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 53,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"          42.5.6   The peer educators on completion of the training shall serve as the focal\
      \ point\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 55,\n      \"text\": \" \
      \                   for any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 56,\n      \"text\": \"                 throughout\
      \ the contract period.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n    \
      \  \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 58,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly honorarium as fixed by the Employer for\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 59,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \         rendering his services in addition to his regular duty.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 60,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 61,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.8   The total number of peer educators (1 for\
      \ 100 workers) shall always be maintained by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 62,\n      \"text\": \"                    the contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 63,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 64,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave the\
      \ contractor by creating vacancy, then\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 65,\n      \"text\": \"                  the contractor at his own expense train the new replacement peer\
      \ educator from\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 66,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 67,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor\
      \ that due care should be taken to select the peer\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 68,\n      \"text\": \"                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain\
      \ with the contractor\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 69,\n     \
      \ \"text\": \"                   throughout the contract period.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 70,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 74,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito breeding\
      \ at site. The measures to be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"         taken shall include:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n   \
      \   \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                i)    Empty cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"            \
      \      shall be deposited at a central collection point and shall be removed from the\"\n    },\n    {\n \
      \     \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"                   site regularly.\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"                  ii)\
      \      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"              mosquito\
      \ breeding.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 13,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \              iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on the site, which may retain water,\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"              \
      \    shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner that water could not be\"\n    },\n    {\n     \
      \ \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"                 retained.\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"             iv)   Water\
      \ storage tanks shall be provided.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 19,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the dangers\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \ of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 25,\n      \"text\": \"42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange\
      \ to prevent mosquito breeding\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"        by fumigation  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"         include\
      \ SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex, The Ideal Larvicide etc.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 28,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 29,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.7     Alcohol and drugs\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 31,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"\

      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"          influence\

      \         regulations.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 39,\n    \
